chi_adm_imperial_law_response_prompt = {
 type = country
 text = {
  trigger = {
   chi_adm_imperial_law_can_answer = yes
  }
  localization_key = chi_adm_imperial_law_choose_response
 }
 text = {
  trigger = {
   always = yes
  }
  localization_key = chi_adm_imperial_law_empty_response
 }
}
chi_adm_imperial_law_eligibility = {
 type = country
 text = {
  trigger = {
   var:chi_adm_imperial_law_status ?= 3
   has_variable = chi_adm_imperial_law_implementation_paused
  }
  localization_key = chi_adm_imperial_law_paused_desc
 }
 text = {
  trigger = {
   var:chi_adm_imperial_law_status = 3
  }
  localization_key = chi_adm_imperial_law_implementation_status
 }
 text = {
  trigger = {
   NOT = {
    has_variable = chi_adm_imperial_law_active
   }
  }
  localization_key = chi_adm_imperial_law_no_active_eligibility
 }
 text = {
  trigger = {
   chi_adm_imperial_law_adopted = yes
  }
  localization_key = chi_adm_imperial_law_already_adopted
 }
 text = {
  trigger = {
   chi_adm_imperial_law_available = no
  }
  localization_key = chi_adm_imperial_law_special_requirements
 }
 text = {
  trigger = {
   enacting_any_law = yes
  }
  localization_key = chi_adm_imperial_law_busy_requirements
 }
 text = {
  trigger = {
   chi_adm_imperial_law_available = no
  }
  localization_key = chi_adm_imperial_law_political_requirements
 }
 text = {
  trigger = {
   always = yes
  }
  localization_key = chi_adm_imperial_law_requirements_met
 }
}

 type = country

  trigger = {
   var:chi_adm_imperial_law_status = 3

  }

   always = yes
